But there are also those who are still alive, over 80 years old and more, who have experienced very difficult times of fighting collectiveism, with the obligation to supply. They also suffered greatly because it was persecution. All I can tell you is about my father-in-law, who was a gimp who was destroyed, who was suffering. Of course, you can tell why. One of those evidences he showed me... He says, "Look at them stalking me." I'm asking you, but what are they haunting you for? Only because he was born in 1917 in Chicago. His parents arrived in Poland in 1921. And for the fact that he was born in Chicago in personal evidence, he was being harassed. Not just for what he preached, but for what he did. And where he showed up, in all the services... Today's sacred memory... May he rest there... But I show many others who have been persecuted. Of course, they're not listed in any I.P.N. files, they're not there. But they, too, are worthy today to value them. There are a lot of farmers coming to my office right now who are asking to be in the IPN as repressed persons. They acted, we know them, but there are no documents in the IPN, because farming was often hidden. The service couldn't always check it out. Our activities, such a more conspiracy activity, showed that you can hide each other, you can hide where there were printed paperwork, correspondence, etc. Everything was hidden and not always the servants got to it. And the I.P.N. doesn't have documents, and these people were being harassed. So I have a request to IPN for an increased job. Because these people put together conclusions and are waiting to be dealt with. They are often refused because there are no documents, it is often said that witnesses are unbelievable, and some people are excluded, and of course they would also like to benefit, because agricultural pensions are the lowest pensions today. So if they, the sick, could still benefit at this stage, at 70 or 80, then I'd be grateful. But there is a request to the IPN and also to the Repressed Office for faster examination of their applications, because they are waiting for this possibility. It's my appeal and request. The Commission asks the High House to adopt the attached draft resolution, the adoption of a bill with amendments proposed by the committee. The High Izbo, which I am talking about here today, was a parliamentary project, a very short one that really concerns one issue, namely the extension of the circle of people who can carry out qualifying tests before vaccination, during the outbreak of COVID-19. I think this change has been expected for a long time. With the vaccination programme submitted in December... Even then, it seemed that there would be a need. The number of vaccination points that should be increased... This also required an extension of the group of individuals qualifying for vaccination. At first, the bill did not raise much doubt, because, as I said, it was expected. After careful reading into the records proposed by the Sejm, during an in-depth discussion, as well as reading into the opinion presented by the legislators, the committee decided to clarify this article and list names... not names, but professional groups of people who can qualify for vaccination. Initially, only a doctor was allowed to qualify for vaccination in the Anti-infection and infectious Disease Act. In the introduction that came to us from the Sejm, this list was extended to include people who do not necessarily pursue a medical profession. This raised a lot of doubt during the discussion at the committee meeting. By supporting, as I say, a legal opinion that has absolutely confirmed the fact that it cannot be so that persons who are not literally mentioned in the law are defined in the regulation, as proposed by the Parliament's draft... After listening to the Minister of Health's position, who confirmed that the Ministry of Health's intention was to pursue specific medical professions, we clarified what the Minister said. It's the same record. A parliamentary amendment was tabled during the work on the Law in the Sejm. This amendment goes absolutely beyond the scope of this law, it does not concern the problem of combating occupational diseases, it only extends the deadlines for existing lists, lists of providers needed later to contract and implement payments by the National Health Fund. This out-of-law misdemeanor, due to the urgency of the time limits, has been considered reasonable, although I would stress that this provision goes beyond the original draft law. The assessment of the Legislative Office strongly stressed that this proposal confirms our position that this is an out-of-law offence, but because of the urgency and the need to keep the deadlines or the extension of these deadlines, the committee was positive and agreed that this article should also remain in this project. However, the Legislative Office has submitted comments which clarify the process of recording these changes themselves. These are legislative changes that have been proposed by our Legislative Office and adopted by the Health Commission. In conclusion, I would like to say that the Health Committee voted positively on this draft, together with the 2 amendments tabled, both the amendment concerning the amendment to Article. 1, i.e. with regard to the law on the fight against infections and infectious diseases, as well as the amendment going beyond the proposed law, and related to the extension of the time limits for lists of providers. I ask the High House to give a positive opinion on the project that has been submitted. Thank you very much.

I would like to remind you that this article prejudges that the regulations are issued by the bodies indicated in the Constitution on the basis of the detailed authorisation provided for in the Act and for its implementation. ParlaMint-PL_2021-03-24-senat-22-1.u366	Thank you, Senator. I inform you that the list of speakers has been exhausted. ParlaMint-PL_2021-03-24-senat-22-1.u367	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present a report to the 3rd Committee on Legal Affairs, the Committee on Local Government and State Administration and the Committee on Foreign Affairs and the European Union Which committees submit to the High House together with a request to adopt the attached draft law on the Cohesion and Development Agency. We had the opportunity and in the Senate forum, and in the public debate to justify the bill. I'll be very brief. The aim is to find the most appropriate institutional form for the programming and management of European funds, which in the forthcoming budgetary agenda will go to Poland and other Member States in an extraordinary amount, so as to ensure their professional use, politically impartial and the most transparent. This involves the concern to maximise the potential of local government, local government units, which were most responsible for the implementation of European projects. The basic idea is to create an independent agency in the legal form of an executive agency based on the principle of cooperation between government and local government, as well as other constitutional institutions, including the Marshal of the Sejm and the Marshal of the Senate, and the parties to social dialogue. It is not, therefore, a matter of taking over the management of such programmes to move from government to self-government, but of entrusting this task, this special task of the institution based on the principle of cooperation between the two parties, i.e. synergies. The Agency would have the main management powers in the field of national operational programmes, and I still use the existing nomenclature, with the exception of programmes relating to fisheries, agricultural policy, agriculture, as well as the asylum and migration fund. The Agency would also manage the use on the basis of European programmes of a special recovery fund after COVID. I also use the well-known category of regional operational programmes, which would be the responsibility of local voivodships. At their request, the Agency could only assist local authorities in carrying out this task. This is a circle of essential competences described in the Act. As I mentioned, the fundamental idea of the project is to entrust this special task to the new institution based on the principle of cooperation and constitutional institutions, and the government party, and the local government party. This dictates the structure of the council, in which both sides, but also those appointed by the Marshal of the Sejm and the Marshal of the Senate, will form its composition, as well as those designated by the Council of Social Dialogue, representing the two sides of the Council of Social Dialogue. The government of this agency will mean the need to communicate with both parties, hence the qualified majority needed for the selection of the board. We assume that there will be a competition procedure in the selection of the persons to whom the management function will be entrusted. During the consultation we accepted the amendments suggested by the Ministry of Finance, these are the regulations of the Article. 9 and 13 concerning the conduct of the financial economy and the revenue of the Agency. Finally, I would like to thank all those who participated in the drafting of the bill, above all Prof. Hubert Izdebski, the senators, all those who signed the original bill, including those who were the most active Senator Krzysztof Kwiatkowski, Senator Zygmunt Frankiewicz, Senator Marcin Bosacki, Senator Filip Libicki, Marshal Stanecka With great power the representatives of the local government who took part in the consultation of the draft law and contributed to the final content of the project. Many proposals have been adopted between the creation of the original version of the project and the preparation of what the High House receives from 3 joint committees. I therefore request that the draft be adopted in the version in which three joint committees report it. Thank you very much for your attention.
